alias cc clear counters
alias cf cle cef drops 
alias cn clear controller npu stats counters-all instance all location 0/0/CPU0
alias ct clear controller npu stats traps-all instance all location 0/0/CPU0
alias fr sh mpls traffic-eng fast-reroute database
alias sn show controller npu stats counters-all instance all location all
alias st show controllers npu stats traps-all instance all location all | ex "0         0"
alias te show mpls traffic-eng tunnels summary
alias fec show controllers npu resources fec location 0/0/CPU0
alias mac show controllers fia diagshell 0 "l2 show" location 0/0/CPU0 | i mac= | util wc -l
alias ball show bfd all | i Sessions
alias ecmp show controllers npu resources ecmp location 0/0/CPU0
alias bgpall sh bgp all all process performance-statistics detail | i "Default VRFs:|Non-Default VRFs:"
alias cfm-peer show ethernet cfm peer meps | i ">  " | utility wc -l
alias int-down show interfaces brief | ex up | util wc -l
alias cef-count sh cef misc  |  in "Num cef entries|protocol debug statistics"
alias cfm-local show ethernet cfm local meps | i Up | util wc -l     
alias mac-count show controllers fia diagshell 0 "l2 show" location 0/0/CPU0 | i mac= | util wc -l
alias clear-lpts cle lpts pifib hardware statistics location 0/0/CPU0

interface preconfigure HundredGigE0/0/1/3
 ipv6 enable
!
rd-set evpn_rd
  65000:1
end-set
!
prefix-set loopback
  100.100.201.251/32,
  100.100.200.0/24 le 32,
  100.100.201.0/24 le 32,
  100.100.100.0/24 le 32
end-set
!
prefix-set bgp_lu_prefix
  52.252.0.0/16 le 32
end-set
!
prefix-set block_vpnv4_scale
  220.1.0.0/16 le 32
end-set
!
prefix-set drop_to_avoid_loop
  100.100.200.0/24 le 32,
  100.100.201.0/24 le 32
end-set
!
prefix-set ASBR-Loopback-filter
  100.100.100.101/32,
  100.100.100.103/32
end-set
!
prefix-set connected_prefix_as1
  192.0.0.0/8 ge 24 le 24
end-set
!
route-policy PASS
  pass
end-policy
!
route-policy ADDPATH
  set path-selection backup 1 install multipath-protect advertise
end-policy
!
route-policy bgp_lu_label
  if destination in loopback then
    pass
  endif
  if destination in bgp_lu_prefix then
    pass
  endif
  if destination in connected_prefix_as1 then
    pass
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy red_connected
  if destination in loopback then
    pass
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy block_vpnv4_scale
  if destination in block_vpnv4_scale then
    drop
  else
    pass
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy allow_evpn_inter_as
  if rd in evpn_rd then
    pass
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy table_policy_avoid_loop
  if destination in drop_to_avoid_loop then
    drop
  else
    pass
  endif
end-policy
!
router static
 address-family ipv4 unicast
  1.1.0.0/16 1.4.0.1
  1.3.0.0/16 1.4.0.1
  1.4.0.0/16 1.4.0.1
  1.5.0.0/16 1.4.0.1
  10.251.1.2/32 HundredGigE0/0/1/0
  10.251.252.2/32 TenGigE0/0/0/2
  10.254.1.1/32 HundredGigE0/0/1/2
  172.28.98.178/32 1.4.0.1
  223.255.254.253/32 1.4.0.1
  223.255.254.254/32 1.4.0.1
 !

  bfd minimum-interval 300
  bfd multiplier 3
  default-information originate
  address-family ipv4 unicast
   label mode per-ce
   maximum-paths ebgp 8
   redistribute connected
   redistribute static
  !
  address-family ipv6 unicast
   label mode per-ce
   maximum-paths ebgp 8
   allow vpn default-originate
   redistribute connected
   redistribute static
  !
 !
